Future BILL-001–015, MSG-001–009, INT-001–009, CHANNEL-001–004, APP-001–005 and MEM-005 carry portable records/documents and paused studio billing/delivery until reviewed human-owner reconnection before shipping. Every later clause adding a record kind, provider or studio door inherits the same obligation.  **Acceptance evidence:** Download the exit bundle and native archive from a populated studio, reconstruct relationships in a fresh receiver, compare totals and documents and confirm no outbound collection or message occurs before reconnection approval.

### Security custody and operating boundaries

- **SEC-001 — Private records and operational recovery.** Studios retain deployment and data custody, encrypted transport, protected provider credentials and least-privilege access, with tested backup recovery, actionable integration diagnostics and clear stale/error states. Logs redact member notes and payment secrets and every export requires explicit rights. **Acceptance evidence:** Deploy to a controlled studio account, exercise role-separated exports and provider failure diagnostics, inspect logs for planted secrets and restore a backup to a recovery installation.

- **SEC-002 — Durable staff and automation audit.** Record actor, role, policy/version, time, source and before/after effect for staff access, member changes, billing attempts, autopay skips and postponements, staff-made contract freezes, freeze ends, terminations and canceled terminations with their type or reason code and comment, cash drawer adjustments, refunds, entitlements, attendance corrections, imports, exports, messages and agent execution. Every staff action on a member's behalf through a STAFF-006 staff door is recorded as staff-made with the acting staff member, role, STAFF-001 right used, member, channel (desk, phone, inbox or staff device) and any reason: bookings, cancellations with their early or late type, fee waiver and notice suppression, moves, restores, waitlist actions, spot and guest bookings, makeups, check-ins, sales and card-on-file charges, invoices and receipts sent, payment collections, retries and payment-method changes, plan changes, freezes and terminations, signing sessions launched, and profile, household, intake, preference, referral and privacy-request records. Authorized owners search/export the audit by member, staff member, right and action; erasure retains only permitted pseudonymous financial evidence. **Acceptance evidence:** Change staff rights, correct attendance, run a renewal and approved refund, export and erase a member; inspect the complete ordered audit and verify agents and staff cannot rewrite it. As front desk, cancel one member's booking late, early-cancel another with a waiver reason, move and restore bookings and charge a card on file; filtering the audit by member and by right shows each action once as staff-made with its right, channel and reason. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 staff-made action records in searchable and exportable durable audit history; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors. STAFF-006 obligation before this capability ships: every member action here must ship its table-mapped staff door and named right, the same preview, policy, notice and once-only identity, any member-owned authorization step, and one canonical staff-made actor/member/channel/before/after entry; assert the member/staff pair through HTTP and the 390-pixel staff view, including denied/revoked rights under STAFF-001. Current partial search/export semantics: the native owner filters existing canonical staff-made history by exact member, acting staff member, right, channel and command and inclusive studio-local from/through dates. Newest UTC event comes first, with event identity as the tie-breaker; the screen, JSON and CSV select the same records. JSON/CSV preserve event identity, time, actor and role, member, command/action, named right, channel, reason and before/after facts; replay payloads and command results are excluded. CSV uses JSON-valued data cells, with before/after objects encoded as JSON text; erased profile values remain null under the existing unused-identity policy. The HTTP doors are read-only and keep existing owner-only authority. This does not claim complete studio auditing or change retention policy. MEM-005 obligation before complete signing audit ships: preserve separately attributable launch, resend, personal completion, authority change and revocation events, with actor, participant, role, named right, channel, time, document identity, device context, authority reference and revision, relevant before and after facts and once-only event identity. Personal-copy requests and delivery outcomes remain distinguishable from signature completion. Credentials stay outside audit records and document content remains in restricted evidence storage.

- **SEC-003 — Member erasure and retention controls.** Owners fulfill access and deletion requests for active, used and imported members, cancel or retain financial obligations explicitly, remove unnecessary notes/documents/provider tokens and preserve legally required records with stated reason and retention date. Erasure receipts and journals fence old backups without pretending to erase external copies. Owners holding the privacy right record a request a member makes in person, by phone or by email, verify the member's identity and fulfill it on their behalf. API-002 obligation before this capability ships: erased people are absent from both `roster:read` and boolean-only `attendance:read`, with every inherited API-002 fence retained. API-003 obligation before this capability ships: a used or imported member's erasure removes their rows from `attendance:read` outcome rows and retires their `memberRef`; assert it through the integration door. **Acceptance evidence:** Request a used member's export and erasure, resolve an outstanding invoice, verify redaction of notes, waivers and provider links under the chosen policy, retain justified financial evidence and refuse resurrection with an old backup and later journal. Record a phoned-in access request, verify identity and deliver the export; the request and its outcome are audited, and a manager without the privacy right is refused. STAFF-006 obligation before this capability ships: every member action here must ship its table-mapped staff door and named right, the same preview, policy, notice and once-only identity, any member-owned authorization step, and one canonical staff-made actor/member/channel/before/after entry; assert the member/staff pair through HTTP and the 390-pixel staff view, including denied/revoked rights under STAFF-001. APP-003 obligation before this capability ships: used-member erasure deletes the member's emergency contact and erases each profile change entry's values exactly as unused-identity erasure does, keeping only actor, role, right, channel, time and changed field names; assert it through the erasure door and a later-journal archive restore. API-002 obligation before this capability ships: a used or imported member's erasure removes their entries from `roster:read`; assert it through the integration door. SCHED-009 obligation before its new door ships: preserve verified/revocable makeup authority, participant-owned adult delegation acceptance, the retained original absence/used visit, explicit credit expiry and one original-linked allocation; no erasure, import or staff grant may create a second makeup credit or expose unrelated child records. MEM-005 obligation before used signing-evidence retention or erasure ships: define justified record classes, retention periods, restricted access, holds and disposal for retained documents, affirmations and personal-copy metadata. A hold records its basis, scope, actor and review or release conditions. Prove used-record handling and later-journal restore behavior without promising erasure of external recipients or offline copies.

- **SEC-004 — Multi-factor sign-in for privileged roles: owners, factors, recovery and reset.** Owners enroll a second factor (passkey or TOTP authenticator) before reaching refunds, payouts, pay, exports, staff rights or provider connections. Recovery codes are single use, a factor reset requires another owner and is audited, and members may opt in. Strap's own factors protect Base sign-in. **Doors:** a person who must have a factor and has none is refused every gated page, form, JSON and download with 403 `mfa_enrollment_required`; ordinary work stays open. Anyone with a working factor (required or opted in) is held at every gated door with `mfa_required` until the session presents one, lasting that session. Gated today: revenue and teaching-pay reports; every CSV, XLSX, text and exit download; staff history; pay rules and every pay view other than an instructor's own; staff rights (inviting, deactivating, reactivating, changing a role, and the owner's finance-report grant); agent keys; integration keys; kiosk pairing; the member-email connection; factor resets. Refunds (BILL-008), payouts (BILL-011), Stripe (BILL-001), payroll and provider connections (STAFF-003–005, INT-001–009, MSG and CHANNEL connections) join before they ship. Agent, integration and kiosk keys never satisfy, perform or bypass a factor check. **Factors:** TOTP RFC 6238 (SHA-1, six digits, 30 seconds, one step either side, each step once per factor), secret sealed AES-256-GCM under a dedicated `MFA_KEY` generated at setup (never the session secret), setup refused while `MFA_KEY` is absent, factor active after one valid code. Passkeys use WebAuthn with user verification required, the installation host as RP, ES256 or RS256, single-use challenges expiring after five minutes, and any attestation accepted without claiming the maker checked. **Enroll/remove:** a first factor needs Base fresh email-link step-up; adding or removing beside a working factor needs the session's check; a person who must have a factor cannot remove their last. **Recovery:** ten codes shown once, hashed, each once in one transaction, counting only while a working factor exists; regenerating after a factor check voids the old set. Five wrong since the last right code pauses that person's checks for 15 minutes. **Reset:** only another owner, in a factor-checked session, after Base step-up and with a required reason; removes factors and recovery codes and ends all their sessions. Nobody resets their own; a sole owner recovers only with a recovery code, with no support or installation bypass. **Audit:** SEC-002 staff history records every enrollment, removal, reset (actor, subject, time, reason, factors removed), recovery-code use, regeneration and pause; never a secret, code or credential. **Archives:** factor secrets, passkeys and recovery hashes never enter a native archive or MOVE-011 exit; a restored studio sends everyone to enrollment, and a changed `MFA_KEY` does the same for authenticator apps with the reason shown. **Finance-report grant:** gives a member read of the revenue and collections report and its downloads only; it is not STAFF-001's finance role, which replaces it when STAFF-001 ships. STAFF-001's manager and finance roles reach gated doors only through SEC-005, and no right grants reset power. SEC-001, SEC-002, SEC-003, MOVE-011, API-002, API-003 and APP-001 inherit these protections before their new doors ship. **Acceptance evidence:** Enroll a passkey and a TOTP app and sign in with each; use one recovery code twice (second refused); have one owner reset another's factor and inspect the audit record. Before enrollment, attempt an owner export, a teaching-pay adjustment, a staff role change and an integration-key creation (each refused with nothing written); verify a replayed TOTP code, a sixth wrong code within 15 minutes, a self-reset and an agent or integration key on a gated door are refused, factor data is absent from a native archive and the exit, and a member who opts in is asked for the factor at each new sign-in.

- **SEC-005 — Multi-factor sign-in for privileged roles: managers, finance staff and payouts.** Managers and finance staff enroll a second factor (passkey or TOTP authenticator) before reaching refunds, payouts, pay, exports, staff rights or provider connections. Every SEC-004 rule applies unchanged to STAFF-001's manager and finance roles and to BILL-011's payout report. **Acceptance evidence:** Invite a finance user and attempt a payout report before enrollment (refused), then enroll and open it; invite a manager and attempt an export before enrollment (refused).

- **BOUNDARY-001 — Payments tax and payroll custody.** Strap owns the studio billing, membership and reconciliation experience. Stripe operates card/ACH rails and holds payment data; Stripe Tax or the selected tax service calculates tax; Gusto/ADP execute approved payroll and filings; see [Envelope](https://github.com/just-every/run-envelope) for payroll; QuickBooks Online or Pantry is the general ledger. Provider setup and agreements are delivery work. **Acceptance evidence:** Complete a studio purchase, tax calculation, pay export and ledger handoff; verify each provider reference and pending/failed outcome is visible without claiming Strap holds funds, files taxes or completed unapproved payroll.

- **BOUNDARY-002 — Studio scope and neighboring products.** Strap owns boutique fitness/yoga studio operations, including youth/family classes, course tuition and member retention. General sales CRM remains Orbit; general subscription billing remains Nectar; signatures beyond studio waivers remain Inkwell; general workflow automation remains Crank. Link these records through integrations instead of merging the products. **Acceptance evidence:** From a studio member or invoice open a linked Orbit/Nectar/Inkwell record through a permitted integration and verify permissions and source ownership; a handoff never creates a second conflicting studio balance.

- **BOUNDARY-003 — Specialist instruction and health boundaries.** Studio intake and class attendance do not diagnose or replace a clinical record, school system or competition platform. Buyers needing belt/stripe curricula, workout performance or recital/costume administration use explicit links and exports to Zen Planner, Wodify or specialist dance software; studio membership, billing and bookings remain in Strap. **Acceptance evidence:** Export a permissioned participant and enrollment as a Zen Planner member-import CSV, import it into a Zen Planner trial account and link its progress record back; restricted intake stays private and no medical, school or specialist-progress parity is asserted.

- **BOUNDARY-004 — Partner delivery and portability evidence.** Native store approval, ClassPass/Wellhub commercial access, provider delivery, legal waiver evidence and payment-method transfer must be completed and demonstrated for production. The target includes that engineering and onboarding. Provider acceptance, human receipt and business outcome remain distinct, and unsupported export fields become migration exceptions. **Acceptance evidence:** Inspect a controlled launch with store distribution, aggregator onboarding and live delivered messages; interrupt one provider and block one token transfer, then verify the unresolved states and member reauthorization path are visible.

## Push further

- **SUCCESS-001 — Detect explainable attendance drop-off.** The member-success agent joins real booking/attendance patterns, membership/freeze state, available classes, cancellations and consent to explain a deviation from a member's own routine. It cites dates and uncertainty, excludes unavailable opportunities and queues outreach under owner frequency limits. **Acceptance evidence:** Run an approved member journey through public booking/attendance doors, remove visits for one routine and freeze another member; inspect cited risk reasons, no false freeze alert and no message without permitted policy. Semantics (SUCCESS-001): native owners and connected preparation keys use the source rules and exclusions in LEAD-004 to prepare immutable explanations of completed weekly class routines. Proposals cite dated attended bookings and classes, elapsed missed opportunities, cancellation and unavailable-week exclusions, current plan and freeze identities, independently recorded channel permission and uncertainty about why visits stopped. No health diagnosis or consent is inferred. Only the native human owner confirms a private outreach-review task after recomputing the booking and membership sources, current channel permission and the explicitly configured per-member rolling 30-day task cap inside the DATA-001 revision fence. Changed sources, permission revisions or limits require a fresh proposal; an opt-out cannot be undone by replaying an old capture. One permanent task identity covers the member, routine and last attended booking, and every confirmed task counts against the cap across channels. AGENT-001 preparation keys cannot confirm, read the private queue or gain private appointment sources through this workflow. Confirmation retains the owner, time, exact evidence and note; identical capture replay retains one task, while a changed replay is refused. The task is private review work and sends no member message or payment. MEM-006 supplies the existing channel-permission rule; AUTO-001 inherits automatic triggers and MSG-001–009 retain actual external delivery and their provider boundaries before any outbound capability ships.

- **SUCCESS-002 — Offer a workable attendance plan.** Using member-provided schedule preferences, travel and location choices, class eligibility, remaining credits, live capacity and actual visits, the agent offers feasible class times and a plan change or freeze request when useful. It never invents availability or diagnoses health. **Acceptance evidence:** Give a member a narrow available schedule and no entitlement to reformer classes; inspect only eligible available choices, accept one and verify the offered class remains feasible after a concurrent booking. STAFF-006 obligation before this capability ships: every member action here must ship its table-mapped staff door and named right, the same preview, policy, notice and once-only identity, any member-owned authorization step, and one canonical staff-made actor, member, channel, before and after entry; assert the member and staff pair through HTTP and the 390-pixel staff view, including denied or revoked rights under STAFF-001. Semantics (SUCCESS-002): the member alone records dated availability intervals and chosen locations. An owner, connected preparation key or explicitly scoped front-desk user prepares at most ten chronological published choices, citing class identities, current eligible packs and actual attended booking identities. The entire class must fit an interval and chosen location; current credits, plan hours, freeze and cancellation state, intake, waiver, booking windows, existing overlapping bookings and online capacity are checked. Offers hold no seat and send nothing. No eligible choice is stated honestly, with the existing member plan-change and freeze request doors; those retain their own human approval requirements. Member acceptance or the booking-right staff pair rechecks current source details and eligibility inside the ordinary booking transaction; a changed preference, changed class or filled seat refuses without partial booking or credit use. Replay preserves one booking and its ordinary confirmation. A separate owner-granted attendance booking scope and enabled member-record scope must both name the member and location on every front-desk request, including replay; second-factor protection applies and revocation is immediate. Staff can start the member sign-in step but cannot set availability. Preferences, scope revisions and source-linked offers remain portable. No health diagnosis, autonomous contract change, refund or outbound campaign is performed. STAFF-001 inherits these named booking scopes and denials for wider roles; SUCCESS-003 retains approved freeze and refund execution, and AUTO-001 and MSG clauses retain future automatic triggers and outreach obligations.

- **SUCCESS-003 — Book accepted help under human control.** After a member accepts a specific class offer the agent books through the ordinary capacity, waiver and entitlement rules and confirms the result. Refunds, debt waivers, plan/contract changes and freezes require the authorized human's approval of exact effects; agents cannot approve their own proposals. **Acceptance evidence:** Accept a suggested class, fill its last seat concurrently and verify a truthful conflict or single booking; request a freeze and refund and require explicit human approval with a recheck before execution. STAFF-006 obligation before this capability ships: every member action here must ship its table-mapped staff door and named right, the same preview, policy, notice and once-only identity, any member-owned authorization step, and one canonical staff-made actor/member/channel/before/after entry; assert the member/staff pair through HTTP and the 390-pixel staff view, including denied/revoked rights under STAFF-001.

- **SUCCESS-004 — Measure recovered visits and renewals.** The agent follows accepted and declined interventions through subsequent attendance, collected renewal revenue and member feedback, separating correlation from demonstrated experiments. Owners approve cohorts, incentives and spending; small or incomplete groups stay inconclusive and members can opt out. **Acceptance evidence:** Run an approved attendance-recovery campaign, record later visits and one failed renewal, compare observed and collected outcomes with source events and inspect an inconclusive small cohort without a fabricated success claim.

- **SUCCESS-005 — Recover billing without breaking access.** The agent compares invoice/payment events, ACH return state and entitlement policy, explains the actual failure and prepares a secure recovery action. Owner-set retries and grace may run deterministically; refunds, waived debt and contract changes require human approval. **Acceptance evidence:** Return an ACH debit after apparent success, ask for recovery help and inspect the provider-grounded explanation, correct debt/access and a secure update link; refuse an agent attempt to waive the balance.

- **SUCCESS-006 — Balance demand and instructor coverage.** The agent joins waitlists, occupancy, attendance, instructor availability and substitution history to propose additional classes, retiming or coverage. Managers approve the exact timetable/staffing change, recheck existing bookings and choose the member notification before publication. Semantics: the connected agent reads cited class/booking IDs and counts of usable seats, reservations, held offers, waiters, attendance and no-shows, together with retained substitution history and recorded availability, then supplies an additional-class, retiming or coverage proposal and its rationale. These are observed counts, not a demand forecast or a claim of recovered visits. Availability uses active instructors, published resource allocations and recorded unavailable intervals with the existing preparation/cleanup buffers and half-open UTC bounds (SCHED-003); absence of a block is not an instructor confirmation. Exception reasons stay owner-only. A frozen recommendation keeps those facts, the exact class, reservation/credit effects and member notice text; changed availability, bookings, qualifications or provider/origin facts require a fresh preview. The ordinary classes.publish door cannot publish a recommendation. Coverage requires the studio's current service qualification and retains substitution provenance. Until STAFF-001 the native human owner acts for the manager; agents only prepare, and STAFF-001 inherits the manager grant, denial and revocation cases before shipping. At approval the owner explicitly selects the shown notification: in-app plus configured email for each affected reservation, or no affected reservations for an addition/empty class. Notice suppression is not offered by this door; provider acceptance is not mailbox delivery, and unavailable or paused email is labelled honestly. Publication preserves current booking IDs, waitlist positions, reserved credits and charges; the same approval never repeats a class, audit or notice. **Acceptance evidence:** Create overfilled and underused classes through public doors, provide instructor availability and request a recommendation; review its cited demand, change availability before approval and require a fresh preview.

- **SUCCESS-007 — Make cutover reconcile.** The migration agent maps actual source members, bookings, plans, credits, signed-waiver evidence, schedules and processor-transfer exceptions, proposes ambiguous matches and explains discrepancies. The owner signs off balances and cutover; the agent cannot activate autopay or infer missing values. **Acceptance evidence:** Upload a real source package with duplicate names, a missing pack balance and an unsupported payment method; inspect cited proposed matches, resolve ambiguities and require owner sign-off before any collection.

- **SUCCESS-008 — Resolve routine member inquiries.** A scoped inbox agent answers from current studio policies, member entitlements, schedules and conversation history, cites its facts and proposes allowed bookings or self-service steps. It escalates uncertainty, sensitive intake, complaints, refund requests and contract changes to people and respects all channel preferences. **Acceptance evidence:** Ask about credits, a class time and a freeze by SMS and in-app, change the source schedule mid-conversation and verify grounded updated answers, approved booking actions and human escalation for the freeze and refund.

## Honest boundaries and delivery obligations

BOUNDARY-001 through BOUNDARY-004 define the boundaries as named, testable product behavior. Strap orchestrates the studio job through named payment, tax, payroll, ledger, access-control, video, messaging and distribution providers. Commercial agreements, app-store release and US sending registration are required implementation work. No provider acknowledgment is treated as human receipt or a recovered visit; no export or token-transfer limitation silently becomes a zero balance or a reduced product.

The wider inventory includes martial-arts curriculum, workout programming and dance recital/costume operations. LIST.json's approved boutique-studio category governs: BOUNDARY-003 provides specialist handoffs for those neighboring workflows while household classes, recurring tuition, makeups and all studio table-stakes remain required here.

## Research basis

The approved 16 October scope and 16 October inventory/code audit are the inputs, with current code rechecked at the status commit. The inventory's broader category does not override the approved boutique-studio category. Vendor names are switching destinations, not a claim of certified integration. Actual file layouts, export entitlements and processor transfers are verified in the named migration acceptance journeys; missing source categories must be reported rather than fabricated. Format examples are adapter obligations, not an assertion that every vendor offers one complete export. Strap accepts CSV, XLS and XLSX through named adapters only after identifying the actual supplied format; vendor documentation saying Excel is not evidence for both extensions. PDF customer lists remain source evidence for reviewed mapping, not a structured-record parity claim. Vendor support/API captures and explicit reconciliation cover missing export categories; unsupported collection methods require member reauthorization, not a shrunken migration.

Migration format references: [Mindbody data exports](https://www.mindbodyonline.com/en-au/business/education/blog/mindbody-contracts-cancellation-data), [Mindbody member report](https://support.mindbodyonline.com/s/article/Members-report-new-Membership-report?language=en_US), [WellnessLiving report exports](https://www.wellnessliving.com/features/advanced-reporting/), [Vagaro Excel customer export](https://support.vagaro.com/hc/en-us/articles/360006371094-Export-Your-Customer-List), [Momence reports](https://help.momence.com/en/articles/12030052-reports-analytics-faqs), [Momence signed-waiver PDFs](https://help.momence.com/en/articles/9535198-download-waivers-as-pdfs), [Arketa CSV reporting](https://help.arketa.com/reporting/overview), [Arketa Stripe account model](https://help.arketa.com/payments/payouts/stripe-overview), [Arketa attendance history limits](https://help.arketa.com/reporting/bookings/attendance), [PushPress transaction CSV/Excel](https://help.pushpress.com/en/articles/14790656-core-transactions-report-how-to-view-and-export-payment-history), [Stripe payment-data migration](https://docs.stripe.com/get-started/data-migrations/pan-import).

The exact-SHA clock journey proves its originally accepted annual interval, elapsed monthly projections, completed finite service, unearned expiry, gift funding refund, supported ordinary finite-pack refund, remaining entitlement, retained original periods and finance screens at 1440 and 390. RPT-004 remains PARTIAL. Missing proof remains for broader changed and frozen service terms, cooling-off and mixed-consideration refunds, excess refunds, unknown valuations, unsupported adjustments, correction-driven report restatement, negative receivables, visible nonzero reconciliation differences, returned gift tender in monthly finance projections and complete exported recognition-field equality. BILL-012 has native classified test tax and reversal coverage, but its recorded real-provider journey lacks exact-SHA clean-tree provenance and does not complete broader tax integration. Policy approval does not prove those missing assertions. SEC-004 retains MFA and its existing explicit finance-report grant. Every original acceptance sentence and inherited duty remains required. Recorded cash is not verified processor settlement; no production tax, filing, legal or accounting certification is claimed. CT Gap clarification, 26 October 2026, reviewed at bd4f6e0c8d8ca2e2889cac760bbd536025da1447: the provisional recognition default, access-only finite-pack freeze classification, bounded ordinary finite-pack refund classification and bounded ordinary unlimited-service cancellation-refund classification are approved with riders; those policy decisions are no longer pending. The exact-SHA finance clock journey demonstrates its accepted annual interval, completed finite service, ordinary supported refunds, stopped time recognition, retained original intervals, partial funding, once-only recorded settlement and associated finance screens at 1440 and 390. RPT-004 remains PARTIAL. Missing assertions remain for classification exclusions and broader changed or frozen service terms and refunds, correction-driven finance restatement, negative receivables, visible nonzero reconciliation differences, returned gift tender in monthly finance projections, complete recognition-field export equality, discriminating rounding cases and direct preservation of unpaid original dues. The real-provider tax comparison still lacks exact-SHA admission provenance. No missing reporting acceptance is carved out by this clarification. Every original acceptance sentence remains required. Historical statements that the annual interval is wholly unproved, all tax classification is unavailable or the approved policy decisions are pending are superseded by this clarification. Implementation clarification, 27 October 2026: the extended existing finance clock journey now asserts mixed consideration, initiation fee, cooling-off and excess-refund exclusions, direct numeric unpaid-dues preservation, complete recognition and ledger export equality, correction-driven reporting, negative receivables, returned gift tender, visible nonzero reconciliation differences and discriminating half-cent and cumulative carry cases. These assertions supersede the corresponding missing-proof inventory in the historical CT clarifications when that journey passes. Remaining classification and reconciliation scope includes changed consideration with refunds, periodic visit-limited cancellation refunds, frozen time-based terms, unknown refund valuations and broader unsupported adjustments. Exact-SHA real-provider tax admission and broader tax integration remain unproved and held for this campaign. No provider, legal or accounting certification is claimed; RPT-004 remains PARTIAL. |
